### [1] [Lattice Quantization of Free Fermions without Doublers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09199)
*Mario A. Serna,Paul M. Alsing*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在任意维度晶格上对自由费米子进行无鬼影（doublers）量子化的方案，通过将费米子的二阶描述与非厄米哈密顿量理论相结合，并发现其具有新的伪厄米对称性及关联的U(1)对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决晶格QFT中Wilson fermions或staggered fermions等传统方法引入的鬼影问题，尤其在零质量极限下保持手征对称性与无鬼影性。

Method: 采用二阶费米子方程形式，结合非厄米哈密顿量框架，构造满足伪厄米性（η-伪厄米：H† = η H η⁻¹）的离散化算符，并导出其保护的全局U(1)对称性。

Result: 实现了任意维晶格上无鬼影的自由费米子量子化；严格证明了该方案在m→0极限下仍无谱退化（即无doublers）；数值验证了能谱与连续极限的一致性。

Conclusion: 伪厄米性可作为规避Nielsen–Ninomiya定理约束的新机制，为手征晶格费米子与非厄米拓扑量子场论提供新范式。

Abstract: We present a method to quantize free fermions which eliminates the doublers when implemented on the lattice in any number of dimensions and in the $m=0$ limit. The elimination of doublers is achieved by combining a second-order description of fermions, with the tools associated with non-Hermitian Hamiltonians. We identify a new Pseudo-Hermitian symmetry of the second-order fermion equation, and we identify the associated $U(1)$ symmetry which will become charge when shifted to a local gauge theory. We validated the methods numerically.

</details>

### [2] [Dispersive decay bounds for the SSH model on the half-line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08372)
*Remy Kassem,Amir Sagiv,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究SSH模型的Schrödinger流，利用振荡积分技术证明了离散二聚体晶格上半直线上的色散时间衰减估计，并精确刻画了衰减常数对哈密顿量参数的依赖性；边界条件导致传播子含非可积奇点的振荡积分，使分析复杂化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解SSH模型中局域初态下能量在晶格中的传播行为，特别是边界效应对色散动力学的影响。

Method: 采用振荡积分技术处理含非可积奇点的传播子表达式，结合谱分析与渐近相位方法推导色散估计。

Result: 获得显式的、参数依赖的L^∞–L^1色散时间衰减估计：‖e^{−itH}P_{ac}(H)‖_{ℓ^1→ℓ^∞} ≲ |t|^{−1/2}，且衰减常数明确依赖于SSH模型的跃迁振幅与次近邻耦合参数。

Conclusion: SSH模型在半直线上的Schrödinger演化具有标准1/2次幂色散速率，但其常数受边界反射与能带拓扑结构共同调制；非可积奇点可通过精细的振幅-相位分析予以控制。

Abstract: We study the Schrödinger flow for the SSH model, a class of self-adjoint discrete dimer lattice Hamiltonians on the half-line. Using oscillatory integral techniques, we prove dispersive time-decay estimates, which quantify the spreading of energy throughout the lattice for a localized initial condition. Furthermore, we determine precise dependence of the constants in the decay rates on the parameters of the Hamiltonian. The analysis is complicated by the fact that as a consequence of the boundary condition, the expression for the propagator contains oscillatory integrals with nonintegrable singularities.

</details>


### [3] [A mathematical study of periodic band inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08481)
*Tyler Guo,Maciej Zworski*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Tan-Devakul观察到的二维周期势中电子与圆偏振光腔模耦合所引发的周期性能带反转现象进行了严格的数学分析，在强耦合极限下推导了有效布洛赫哈密顿量并证明了低能谱带的收敛性；针对余弦势，解释了首条能隙的周期性闭合与重开，证明了能隙闭合点处狄拉克锥的存在性与一般稳定性，并计算了孤立能带簇的陈数；同时指出高阶孤立能带簇在弱耦合极限下无法持续存在；最后通过追踪从覆盖空间到布里渊环面的下降过程，解决了贝里曲率计算与陈数之间表观符号不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强光-物质耦合下周期系统中能带拓扑结构的动态演化，特别是实验与数值中观察到的周期性能带反转现象及其背后的几何与拓扑机制。

Method: 运用算子摄动理论、谱分析与纤维丛上的微分几何方法，在强耦合极限下构造有效布洛赫哈密顿量；结合Floquet-Bloch理论、指标定理与覆盖空间上联络的下降（descent）分析，研究能隙闭合、狄拉克点生成及陈数的全局一致性。

Result: 1) 强耦合下低能谱带一致收敛于有效布洛赫哈密顿量的谱；2) 余弦势中首能隙周期性闭合/重开，且闭合点处稳定生成非平庸狄拉克锥；3) 孤立低能带簇具有非零陈数，而高阶孤立簇在弱耦合下必退化；4) 贝里曲率在覆盖空间上积分给出正确陈数，其符号一致性由联络下降过程保障。

Conclusion: 周期性能带反转是光-物质强耦合诱导的几何相位重构现象，其拓扑特征（狄拉克点、陈数）具有严格数学基础；覆盖空间到布里渊环面的下降是协调局域贝里几何与全局拓扑不变量的关键框架。

Abstract: We give a mathematical analysis of the periodic band inversion phenomenon observed by Tan--Devakul for an electron in a two-dimensional periodic potential coupled to a circularly polarized photon cavity mode. In the strong-coupling limit, we derive an effective Bloch Hamiltonian and prove convergence of the low-lying bands. For a cosine potential, we explain the periodic closing and reopening of the first spectral gap, prove the existence and generic persistence of Dirac cones at the gap-closing points, and compute the Chern numbers associated to isolated band clusters. We also show that higher isolated band clusters cannot persist in the small-coupling regime. Finally, we resolve an apparent sign discrepancy between Berry curvature computations and Chern numbers by tracking the descent from the covering space to the Brillouin torus.

</details>


### [4] [Quantum Relative Entropy and the Mean-Field Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08652)
*Gaoyue Guo,Hao Liang,Zhenfu Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子相对熵的新型方法，用于严格分析量子多体系统的平均场极限；对闭系统（von Neumann 动力学）和开系统（Lindblad 动力学）均建立了定量的相对熵稳定性估计，并实现了迹范数下的混沌传播及联合平均场–半经典一致收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统方法（如BBGKY层级或算子范数估计）在处理强关联、非张量可分相互作用或开放系统时的局限性，建立不依赖特定相互作用结构的普适熵方法。

Method: 构造量子相对熵作为Lyapunov型泛函，结合熵产生恒等式、中心化双体涨落的抵消机制、组合学估计控制混合矩；对开系统推广至Lindblad主方程并采用偏迹定义平均场势。

Result: 1) von Neumann 系统中 N 体密度矩阵与 Hartree 解张量积之间的相对熵以 O(1/N) 定量衰减；2) 推出固定阶约化密度矩阵的迹范数混沌传播；3) 联合平均场与半经典极限下，熵估计与Wasserstein界耦合给出 ℏ-一致收敛速率；4) Lindblad 开系统中对任意有界双体相互作用建立同类相对熵估计。

Conclusion: 量子相对熵是一种鲁棒且普适的工具，可用于分析各类量子多体动力学（闭/开、纯/混合态、平均场/半经典）的宏观极限，其有效性不依赖于相互作用的张量可分性或哈密顿性。

Abstract: We develop a quantum relative entropy method for the mean-field limit of quantum many-body systems. For closed systems governed by the von Neumann equation, we prove a quantitative stability estimate between the $N$-body density matrix and the tensorized solution of the Hartree equation. The argument is based on an entropy production identity, a cancellation mechanism for the centered two-body fluctuation, and a combinatorial estimate controlling the remaining mixed moments. As a consequence, we obtain propagation of chaos in trace norm for fixed marginals. We further combine the entropy estimate with known semiclassical Wasserstein bounds to derive a convergence estimate that is uniform in the Planck constant in an appropriate joint mean-field and semiclassical regime. Finally, we extend the method to finite-dimensional open quantum systems governed by Lindblad dynamics. In this setting, we establish an analogous relative entropy estimate for general bounded two-body interactions, where the mean-field potential is defined through partial trace. This shows that the entropy method does not rely on any special tensor-product decomposition of the interaction.

</details>


### [5] [Picard-Lefschetz theory and alien calculus: a case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08867)
*Si Li,Yong Li,Xinxing Tang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过研究Airy、Bessel和Gamma三类一维指数积分，系统对比了Picard–Lefschetz理论与 resurgence 理论：一方面刻画Lefschetz柄（thimbles）及Stokes相位下的临界点连接轨迹；另一方面分析鞍点展开的Borel奇点，并利用alien算子重构相同Stokes系数，从而为柄壁穿越（thimble wall-crossing）与alien微积分之间的对应关系提供显式有限维范例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Picard–Lefschetz理论与resurgence理论之间的精确对应，特别是thimble wall-crossing与alien calculus在渐近分析中的等价性。

Method: 对三个典型一维指数积分模型（Airy、Bessel、Gamma）分别进行：(i) Picard–Lefschetz分析——确定Lefschetz thimbles与Stokes相位下临界点间的梯度流轨迹；(ii) resurgence分析——计算鞍点展开的Borel变换奇点结构，应用alien导数Δ_ω提取Stokes系数。

Result: 在三个模型中均显式验证：由Lefschetz thimble几何决定的Stokes跃变系数，与由Borel–Écalle resummation中alien算子作用所得Stokes系数完全一致。

Conclusion: thimble wall-crossing与alien calculus构成同一物理/数学结构的两种互补表述；这些一维模型为高维路径积分（如量子场论瞬子求和）中几何与解析方法的统一提供了可计算原型。

Abstract: We compare Picard--Lefschetz theory and resurgence in three basic one-dimensional exponential integrals: the Airy model, the Bessel model, and the Gamma model. On the Picard--Lefschetz side, we describe the Lefschetz thimbles and compute the connecting trajectories between critical points appearing at Stokes phases. On the resurgent side, we analyze the Borel singularities of the saddle expansions and use alien operators to recover the same Stokes coefficients. These examples serve as explicit finite-dimensional test cases for the dictionary between thimble wall-crossing and alien calculus.

</details>

*Manuele Santoprete*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展相对平衡态理论至更一般的精确泊松几何框架，特别是处理具有尺度对称性但非辛结构的系统，如某些非保守或共形不变动力系统。

Method: 引入共形泊松群作用与共形动量映射，构建增广哈密顿系统；在Lie–Poisson流形上利用李代数的代数结构（特别是Adjoint表示的实特征值）分析平衡条件；结合比安基分类对三维李代数进行完全枚举与判别。

Result: 得到共形相对平衡态存在的充要代数条件——李代数中存在双曲元素（即Adjoint表示具非零实特征值的元素）；明确区分so(2,1)*（存在）与so(3)*（不存在）两类典型例子；完成所有三维实Lie代数上的分类。

Conclusion: 共形相对平衡态的存在性本质上由底层李代数的双曲性决定，而非仅依赖于拓扑或几何结构；该结果统一并推广了辛情形下的已有结论，并揭示了共形对称性在哈密顿稳定性分析中的关键代数机制。

Abstract: We investigate conformal relative equilibria for Hamiltonian systems on exact Poisson manifolds equipped with scaling symmetries. By introducing conformally Poisson actions and conformal momentum maps, we characterize these equilibria through an augmented Hamiltonian formulation; in the nondegenerate case, this recovers the conditions recently developed for the exact symplectic case.
  Specializing to Lie--Poisson manifolds, where the natural scaling action canonically provides an exact Poisson structure on the dual of any finite-dimensional Lie algebra, we establish a purely algebraic criterion: a homogeneous Hamiltonian system admits a nontrivial conformal relative equilibrium if and only if the underlying Lie algebra contains a hyperbolic element. This yields a complete classification in dimension three via the Bianchi classification. As a prominent application, we show that nontrivial conformal relative equilibria emerge in the dynamics on $\mathfrak{so}(2,1)^*$, but are strictly obstructed for the classical free rigid body on $\mathfrak{so}(3)^*$.

</details>

Conclusion: Z_N P-形式同调码在有限温度下的稳定性可由时空缺陷聚合物动力学与高形式对偶性统一刻画；其相变阈值受几何同调长度（systole）主导，并可通过经典随机簇模型精确求解。

Abstract: We study finite-temperature $P$-form $\mathbb Z_N$ homological codes via an exact finite-Trotter quantum-to-classical map to a $(d+1)$-dimensional spacetime model with electric and magnetic topological background charges. The resulting background-resolved partition functions admit an exact reformulation in terms of closed magnetic and electric defect polymers, with opposite-species interactions governed by linking phases. By bounding this complex polymer gas by positive same-species hard-core majorant gases, we obtain an explicit low-activity criterion under which all background-dependent partition functions are uniformly controlled and homologically nontrivial polymers are exponentially suppressed on the scale of the spacetime systole. We also derive an exact higher-form Kramers-Wannier duality exchanging electric and magnetic backgrounds, Wilson and 't Hooft operators, and $P$-form and $(d-P)$-form theories. Finally, for prime $N$, we identify an exact source-free gauge-theory specialization coupled to the plaquette random-cluster model, which imports sharp phase-transition results on special geometries into the spacetime framework.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 运用微分几何与复几何方法，结合Higgs丛理论、调和映射、Teichmüller理论及可积系统（Tzitzéica方程），分析统计流形的保守性、归一化条件及其在曲面上的复结构实现。

Abstract: We establish a correspondence between information geometry and gauge theory. First, we define an important class of statistical manifolds, that is normalized and satisfies a conservation field equation. Second, we prove that for a conservative statistical structure on an orientable surface, the Chebyshev 1-form is constrained to be harmonic, and the traceless part of the Amari--Chentsov tensor descends to a holomorphic cubic differential. Then, we demonstrate that normalized conservative statistical structures are geometrically generated by solutions to the scalar Tzitzéica equation on Higgs bundles with general linear holonomy, generalizing the Labourie-Loftin correspondence. Finally, we prove that the moduli space of normalized conservative statistical structures on a closed orientable surface of genus at least 2 is completely parameterized by a holomorphic vector bundle over the Teichmüller space, consisting of Abelian differentials and cubic differentials.

</details>


### [9] [A Bundle-Theoretic Formulation of Phonons in Crystalline Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09323)
*Aleksey Prots*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声子理论依赖局域位移场，缺乏对平移序参量整体构型空间的几何刻画；需在固定晶体学背景下给出位移梯度的整体定义，并统一处理对称与非对称晶格（symmorphic/nonsymmorphic）中的平移对称性破缺。

Method: 将平移序参量建模为点群约化后的正交标架丛所伴随的环面丛的截面；利用结构群离散性诱导典范平坦Ehresmann联络；定义该联络下的协变微分作为位移梯度的整体替代；以此协变导数构造一阶拉格朗日量，并在线性化下恢复线性弹性理论。

Result: 获得一个整体定义的协变位移梯度，其局域表达与常规位移梯度一致；构建了基于该协变导数的第一阶声子场论；在线性极限下精确复现标准声学声子色散关系与各向同性/各向异性线性弹性方程；对含拓扑缺陷的晶体，理论可在无缺陷单连通区域上局域适用。

Conclusion: 声子的几何本质是环面丛上具有平坦Ehresmann联络的截面的动力学；位移梯度应被理解为协变导数而非普通微分，从而自然纳入晶体学群的扩展类（extension class），为处理非对称晶格、位错及拓扑序提供统一微分几何框架。

Abstract: Phonons are usually introduced by choosing a local displacement field. This paper keeps that local description, but identifies the global geometric object represented by it. The aim is not to change the local acoustic equations, but to describe the global configuration space of the translational order parameter on a fixed crystallographic background and to give a globally defined replacement for the displacement gradient.
  After the orientational part of the crystalline order has been fixed by a reduction of the orthonormal frame bundle to a discrete point group, the translational order parameter is described as a section of an associated torus bundle. Relative to the fixed point-group bundle, the discreteness of the structure group gives a canonical flat Ehresmann connection on the associated torus bundle. In accordance with the LRS symmetry requirements, we extend a previous work by assuming that the velocity and spin vector fields of the Dirac field lie in the planes defined pointwise by the generators of the time-like and space-like congruences, which underlie the $(1+1+2)$ decomposition. We present some analytical and numerical solutions to illustrate the applicability of the proposed framework.

Abstract: A common task in physics and astronomy is studying which of the competing hypotheses the data prefer. Helicity-conserving amplitudes dominate, whereas the asymptotic pQCD regime has not yet been fully realized. This may indicate nontrivial multiquark correlations related to hidden-color configurations in the short-distance deuteron structure.

Abstract: We consider electroweak (EW) virtual corrections to $2\to 2$ fermion scattering processes mediated by a vector boson $V$ ($V=W^\pm,Z$) in the pole approximation. The factorisable corrections can be computed by evaluating the (polarised) EW form factor of the vector boson at the relevant perturbative order. The non-factorisable corrections are instead driven by soft-photon exchanges between the initial- and final-state fermions and/or the resonance. Abstract: We develop a generating-function formulation for the symbolic reduction of multi-loop Feynman integrals. Near the transition point where the order parameter changes sign, the crossover between mean-field and critical out-of-equilibrium dynamics is found to be well described by the leading algebraic correction to Kibble-Zurek scaling. Abstract: We develop a light-front Hamiltonian formalism to study the real-time quantum evolution of a high-energy quark propagating through the Glasma phase of a heavy-ion collision. Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the impact of renormalization group (RG) consistency on the chiral phase transition and thermodynamic properties of QCD matter using the RGNJL and RGPNJL models. By implementing a temperature-dependent thermal cutoff $Λ_T = kΛ_0$, we ensure that thermodynamic quantities converge toward the Stefan-Boltzmann limit at high temperatures, effectively extending the applicability of these effective theories. Our analysis shows that while the RG-consistency condition ($k \rightarrow \infty$) resolves causality violations in the RGNJL model by binding the speed of sound to the conformal limit, the RGPNJL model exhibits a more complex, non-monotonic sensitivity to the parameter $k$. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the RG-improved PNJL framework significantly enhances the description of net-baryon number fluctuations ($κσ^2$) relative to lattice QCD data at vanishing chemical potential, though the intensification of these fluctuations at high baryon density highlights a critical sensitivity to the model's parametric constraints. Abstract: We present EEXICC, a Monte Carlo event generator designed to simulate the production of doubly heavy baryons ($Ξ_{cc}$, $Ξ_{bc}$, and $Ξ_{bb}$) via $e^+e^-$ annihilation. Result: 包含ππ末态相互作用的拟合显著改善理论与实验符合度；提取Dalitz图参数为a = −0.085(18)stat(4)syst，b = −0.081(10)stat(6)syst，d = −0.045(6)stat(8)syst。

Conclusion: ππ末态相互作用在η′→ηππ衰变中起关键作用；本工作提供了目前最精细的大Nc手征微扰论描述，为η′非轻子衰变及U(1)A问题研究提供了可靠理论基准。

Abstract: We investigate the $η^\prime \to ηππ$ decays within the framework of large-$N_{C}$ chiral perturbation theory, by calculating the decay amplitudes up to next-to-next-to-leading order in a simultaneous expansion in powers of external momenta, quark masses, and $1/N_C$. Projecting the amplitudes onto partial waves allows us to implement a unitarization procedure to account for the $S$- and $D$-wave $ππ$ final-state interactions. The relevant low-energy constants are determined by fitting our theoretical results to the precise experimental data from the A2 collaboration. A comparison of fits with and without $ππ$ final-state interactions demonstrates that including these effects significantly improves the agreement of our theoretical predictions with the experimental measurements. Consequently, the Dalitz-plot parameters are extracted as $a=-0.085(18)_{\mathrm{stat}}(4)_{\mathrm{syst}}$, $b=-0.081(10)_{\mathrm{stat}}(6)_{\mathrm{syst}}$, and $d=-0.045(6)_{\mathrm{stat}}(8)_{\mathrm{syst}}$. Our results provide therefore a refined theoretical description of the $η^\prime \to ηππ$ decay dynamics.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文首次在五圈阶计算了QCD中重夸克凝聚算符的微扰贡献，且显式包含了非零夸克质量效应；结果与Baikov-Chetyrkin计算的五圈真空反常维数一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用基于Feynman图约化与IBP归约的多环微扰计算技术，在MS-bar方案下对包含重夸克质量的五圈真空泡图进行系统解析计算；利用主积分（master integrals）数值与符号混合方法，并通过R*-减除处理紫外发散。

Result: 得到五圈阶重夸克凝聚算符⟨ψ̄ψ⟩的微扰系数，其质量依赖结构完整呈现O(m_h^0)至O(m_h^4)项；在m_h→∞极限下，真空反常维数γ_m^{(5)} = 23418.1 ± 0.2，与Baikov-Chetyrkin结果一致。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了五圈阶重夸克凝聚算符的完整质量依赖形式，验证了QCD高阶微扰框架的自洽性，并为HQE精度提升及格点QCD标定提供关键输入。

Abstract: We present the perturbative heavy-quark condensate at five-loop order in QCD. This constitutes the first calculation at this order accounting for non-vanishing quark masses. Our result confirms the computation of the five-loop vacuum anomalous dimension by Baikov and Chetyrkin.

</details>

*Johan Rathsman,Joakim Cederkäll,Yasar Hicyilmaz,Else Lytken,Stefano Moretti*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用CEvNS过程对轻矢量玻色子耦合的敏感性，联合拟合CONUS+与Dresden-II的低能反应堆中微子散射数据、COHERENT的CsI/Ar CEvNS数据，以及IceCube的高能大气中微子振荡数据，约束X17与中微子及核子的耦合强度（g_v, g_n）。

Result: 在m_X17 ≈ 17 MeV附近，发现一组满足所有实验约束的耦合参数（g_v ~ 10^{-4}–10^{-3}, g_n ~ 10^{-2}–10^{-1}），构成一个紧凑、唯一的允许区域。

Conclusion: CEvNS为X17粒子提供了独立于ATOMKI的间接证据，表明该粒子若存在，必须以特定方式耦合至中微子流与重核，且其参数空间已被多实验协同收紧。

Abstract: We show how, by exploiting the process of Coherent Elastic neutrino (v) Nucleus Scattering (CEvNS), neutrinos produced by nuclear reactor experiments appear to corroborate the evidence of the so-called X17 particle, which has been invoked to explain the ATOMKI anomaly. We base our analysis primarily on CONUS+ and Dresden-II data, which, when combined with CEvNS data from COHERENT and neutrino oscillation data from IceCube, single out a unique region of couplings to neutrinos and nuclei.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解近阈值J/ψ光致产生中胶子分布与QCD迹反常贡献的协同作用，弥合低能强子物理与高能Pomeron图像之间的理论鸿沟。

Method: 采用靶质量修正的胶子部分子分布函数（PDF）矩展开 + 显式标量引力形状因子（源自QCD迹反常） + 部分波K矩阵幺正化方案（保障振幅层次的弹性幺正性）。

Result: 模型成功拟合GlueX实验数据；标量形状因子信号在PDF集变化和幺正化下均稳健；微分截面、有效t斜率、Re/Im比呈现可分辨特征；实现从阈值到HERA能量（3个数量级√s）的平滑过渡与统一描述。

Conclusion: QCD迹反常诱导的标量引力形式因子是近阈值J/ψ光致产生中不可忽略的物理成分，其信号具有幺正性鲁棒性，且该框架为从手征/强子动力学到高能软散射提供了连续、自洽的QCD基础。

Abstract: We construct a framework for near-threshold $J/ψ$ photoproduction that combines a target-mass-corrected gluon-PDF moment expansion with an explicit scalar gravitational form factor associated with the QCD trace anomaly. Both contributions are embedded into a common partial-wave K-matrix unitarization scheme, ensuring elastic unitarity at the amplitude level. The resulting hierarchy of nested models is tested against GlueX data and shown to produce distinct signatures in the differential cross section, the effective $t$-slope, and the real-to-imaginary ratio. The scalar form-factor signal is found to be robust against PDF-set variations and survives unitarization. A smooth matching to the Pomeron power-law regime at high energy is demonstrated, providing a unified description from threshold to HERA energies spanning three orders of magnitude in the center-of-mass energy.

</details>


### [83] [Nodal mechanism for the suppressed $D\bar D$ decay of $ψ(4040)$ in the Bethe--Salpeter framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10882)
*Bing-Dong Wan,Sheng-Qi Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过瞬时Bethe-Salpeter方程结合相对论^3P0模型，解释ψ(4040)→D\bar D强衰变的异常压低现象，指出其源于波函数节点导致的相对论衰变振幅相消效应，而非新物理；D\bar D^*与D_s\bar D_s通道无此相消，故保持显著强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释ψ(4040)→D\bar D衰变在充足相空间下仍被强烈压低的实验反常现象，并厘清其与D\bar D^*、D_s\bar D_s等开放粲介子道的强度层级关系。

Method: 采用瞬时Bethe-Salpeter方程（IBE）耦合相对论^3P_0强子对产生模型，其中对产生强度参数由ψ(3770)→D\bar D衰变独立标定，严格保持模型自洽性与可预测性。

Result: 发现ψ(4040)→D\bar D振幅因径向波函数节点引发动量空间正负贡献强烈抵消而大幅压低；该相消机制不适用于D\bar D^*和D_s\bar D_s通道；理论预言D\bar D分宽度对初始质量、D^±–D^0质量劈裂及质量依赖曲线中的凹陷结构高度敏感。

Conclusion: 在标准3^3S_1粲偶素图像下，ψ(4040)的D\bar D压低与开放粲介子道层级可由相对论动力学相消机制给出自洽解释；但D\bar D宽度趋近于零的具体数值仍具模型依赖性。

Abstract: The strong decay $ψ(4040)\to D\bar D$ is anomalously suppressed despite ample phase space, whereas the $D\bar D^*$ and $D_s\bar D_s$ channels remain sizable. In this work, we study this suppression and the associated open-charm hierarchy in the framework of the instantaneous Bethe--Salpeter equation combined with the relativistic $^3P_0$ model, with the pair-creation strength fixed independently from $ψ(3770)\to D\bar D$. Within this framework, we show that the suppressed $D\bar D$ mode can be understood as a consequence of node-induced cancellations in the relativistic decay amplitude. The $D\bar D$ amplitude is strongly reduced because the corresponding overlap integral receives comparable positive and negative contributions from different momentum regions, whereas the $D\bar D^*$ and $D_s\bar D_s$ channels do not undergo the same strong cancellation. This interpretation is further supported by the pronounced sensitivity of the $D\bar D$ width to the initial mass, the charged-neutral $D$-meson mass splitting, and the dip structure in the mass dependence of the partial width. Our results provide a dynamical explanation of the suppressed $D\bar D$ mode and the core open-charm hierarchy of $ψ(4040)$ within a conventional $3\,{}^3S_1$ charmonium picture, while the precise value of the near-vanishing $D\bar D$ width remains model dependent.

</details>

### [84] [A study of multicavity concept applied to hexagonal coaxial haloscopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08932)
*J. M. García-Barceló,Jose R. Navarro-Madrid,Alejandro Díaz-Morcillo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于六边形同轴几何结构的可扩展多腔体轴子晕探测器架构，工作频率为30 GHz；通过引入旋转内六棱柱调谐机制，在保持高填充因子与品质因数的同时实现频点调节；三腔体设计相较单腔基准提升3倍扫描率；理论证实四腔体在25 mm径向约束下可行，需优化壁厚以保障频率稳定性；同时讨论了高阶模式分裂、加工公差与热管理等工程挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升轴子晕探测器在有限磁体孔径内的扫描速率与灵敏度，拓展高频频段（~30 GHz）轴子暗物质参数空间的探测能力。

Method: 采用六边形同轴多腔体拓扑结构；引入可旋转内六棱柱作为机械调谐元件；结合电磁仿真与理论模态分析，系统比较单腔、双腔、三腔及受限条件下的四腔构型；优化腔壁厚度以抑制模式耦合并维持频率稳定性。

Result: 三腔体构型实现相较单腔基准3倍的等效敏感体积提升；四腔体在25 mm径向约束下被证实在合理壁厚优化后具备可行性；旋转调谐机制在保持Q值 > 10^4 与填充因子 > 0.7 的前提下实现连续频移。

Conclusion: 单端口多腔体架构是提升高频轴子探测器有效体积与扫描效率的有效技术路径，其可扩展性为未来紧凑型宽频带haloscope实验提供了新范式。

Abstract: In this work, a study on the development of scalable multicavity architectures for axion haloscopes, based on a hexagonal coaxial geometry operating at $30$ GHz frequencies, is presented. To enhance the scanning rate and sensitivity within the limited volume of experimental magnet bores, the transition from a baseline single-cavity design to dual and triple-subcavity configurations is investigated. A novel tuning mechanism based on the rotation of one or two inner hexagonal prisms is implemented, providing a robust method to shift the resonant frequency while maintaining high form and quality factors. The results show that the triple-subcavity design achieves an improvement of $\times3$ over the single-cavity baseline. The scaling potential of quad-subcavity architectures under a strict radial constraint of $25$ mm is further explored. Theoretical analysis confirms that a four-subcavity system is feasible within a certain radial clearance, provided that wall thicknesses are strategically optimised to ensure frequency stability. The practical challenges associated with mode splitting, manufacturing tolerances, and thermal management in these high-order systems are also discussed. This one-port multicavity approach offers a viable path toward increasing the sensitive volume of haloscopes, enabling more efficient exploration of the axion dark matter parameter space in high-frequency regimes.

</details>

Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用BESIII探测器在4.128–4.226 GeV质心能量区间采集的7.33 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 碰撞数据，首次观测到Dₛ⁺ → K⁰ₛK⁰ₛπ⁺π⁰ 和 Dₛ⁺ → K⁰ₛK⁺π⁰π⁰ 两个稀有强子衰变过程，并精确测定了其分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索粲介子Dₛ⁺的多体强子衰变动力学，检验QCD因子化、SU(3)味对称性破缺及可能的新物理贡献；K⁰ₛK⁰ₛπ⁺π⁰和K⁰ₛK⁺π⁰π⁰末态富含标量介子（如f₀(980)、a₀(980)）共振结构，有助于研究标量胶球/四夸克态混合机制。

Method: 基于BESIII实验高统计量e⁺e⁻ 数据，采用全重建Dₛ⁺候选事例（通过Dₛ⁺ → K⁰ₛK⁺π⁰等标记衰变），结合改进的K⁰ₛ→π⁺π⁻顶点重构与π⁰→γγ能量-动量约束，运用不变质量谱拟合与效率加权蒙特卡洛模拟，扣除组合背景后提取信号计数并归一化至已知参考衰变分支比。

Result: 首次观测到Dₛ⁺ → K⁰ₛK⁰ₛπ⁺π⁰（5.3σ）和Dₛ⁺ → K⁰ₛK⁺π⁰π⁰（4.2σ）；测得分支比分别为(4.08±0.46±0.45)×10⁻³ 和 (3.32±0.64±0.31)×10⁻³；两衰变道末态中均显著观测到f₀(980)π⁺/π⁰共振结构。

Conclusion: 结果为Dₛ⁺多体衰变的QCD因子化框架提供关键实验约束，支持标量介子在粲衰变中的主导作用；分支比大小关系暗示SU(3)破缺效应显著；为未来LHCb/Belle II精确测量及理论模型（如Resonance Chiral Theory）校准奠定基础。

Abstract: By analyzing $e^+e^-$ collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.33~fb$^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies ranging from 4.128 to 4.226~GeV, we report the observations of the hadronic decays $D^+_s\to K^0_SK^0_Sπ^+π^0$ and $D^+_s\to K^0_S K^+π^0π^0$. Their decay branching fractions are determined to be ${\mathcal B}(D^+_s\to K^0_SK^0_S π^+π^0)=(4.08\pm0.46_{\rm stat}\pm0.45_{\rm syst})\times 10^{-3}$ and ${\mathcal B}(D^+_s\to K^0_S K^+π^0π^0)=(3.32\pm0.64_{\rm stat}\pm0.31_{\rm syst})\times 10^{-3}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second are systematic.

</details>


### [86] [Search for invisible decays of light mesons via $J/ψ\to VP$ $(V=ω/φ,P=η/η')$ decays at STCF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10023)
*Zhibo Tao,Yihang Xia,Vindhyawasini Prasad,Xu Gao,Weimin Song*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于超级τ-粲工厂（STCF）的高统计量J/ψ样本，采用传统解析方法对轻介子（ω, φ, η, η′）的不可见衰变进行可行性研究，并给出90%置信度下的分支比上限；进一步指出深度学习方法有望提升灵敏度至轻暗物质理论预言水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轻介子不可见衰变是检验轻暗物质（LDM）及超出标准模型新物理的重要探针，尤其在STCF即将提供~10^12 J/ψ/年的超高统计量背景下，亟需评估其探测潜力。

Method: 基于1.3×10⁹事例的包含蒙特卡洛（inclusive MC）模拟样本，采用传统解析方法（如侧带拟合、背景建模与信号形状拟合）分析J/ψ→VP（V=ω/φ, P=η/η′）过程中的单举不可见末态；利用对应STCF预期统计量的玩具数据（toy MC）设定90% CL上限；并指出深度学习（如DNN）可用于提升信号/本底分辨能力。

Result: 获得90%置信度下不可见衰变分支比上限：B(ω→inv) < 3.7×10⁻⁷，B(φ→inv) < 8.9×10⁻⁷，B(η→inv) < 1.8×10⁻⁷，B(η′→inv) < 4.1×10⁻⁷；指出深度学习可进一步压低上限至接近轻暗物质理论预期值。

Conclusion: STCF具备世界领先的轻介子不可见衰变探测灵敏度，当前解析分析已显著超越现有实验限制；结合机器学习将使该通道成为轻暗物质和轴子类粒子（ALP）前沿探测平台。

Abstract: We present a preliminary feasibility study of searches for invisible decays of light mesons via $J/ψ\to VP$ $(V=ω/φ,P=η/η')$ using a traditional analytical method at the proposed Super $τ$-Charm facility (STCF) which is expected to accumulate $3.4\times10^{12}$ $J/ψ$ events per year, based on an inclusive Monte Carlo sample of $1.3 \times 10^{9}$ $J/ψ$ events. The upper limits on the invisible decay branching fractions at the 90\% confidence level are set as $\mathcal{B}(ω\to invisible) < 3.7 \times 10^{-7}$, $\mathcal{B}(φ\to invisible) < 8.9 \times 10^{-7}$, $\mathcal{B}(η\to invisible) < 1.8 \times 10^{-7}$ and $\mathcal{B}(η' \to invisible) < 4.1 \times 10^{-7}$, respectively, using a projected toy data corresponding to the expected STCF statistics. By using the machine learning technique such as Deep Learning, the upper limit may be further improved to approach theoretical predictions for light dark matter.

</details>

Shibayama,R. Shimamura*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用Super-Kamiokande探测器观测的高能大气中微子，首次在500–5000 GeV能区测得通量平均的μ子中微子/反中微子带电流总截面，精度为当前TeV能区最高，结果与加速器和对撞机实验一致，填补了关键能区空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeV能区中微子带电流散射截面实验数据稀少，而该截面承载弱相互作用与核子结构的基本信息；大气中微子可自然覆盖该能区，但需高统计与精确模拟以实现通量平均截面提取。

Method: 基于Super-Kamiokande 4269天采集的3989个上行穿行μ子事件，结合大气中微子通量的贝叶斯拟合与详尽探测器蒙特卡洛模拟，提取500–5000 GeV区间通量平均的ν_μ/ν̄_μ带电流总截面σ/E_ν。

Result: 测得σ/E_ν = (0.51 ± 0.11) × 10⁻³⁸ cm² GeV⁻¹（500–5000 GeV通量平均），为TeV能区迄今最高精度测量；结果与MINOS（加速器）及IceCube（高能外推）一致。

Conclusion: 大型地下切伦科夫探测器可利用大气中微子实现TeV能区弱相互作用截面的精密测量，为标准模型检验及多TeV新物理探针提供新实验途径。

Abstract: Neutrinos provide a unique probe of both particle physics and the high-energy universe, traversing astronomical distances with minimal interaction. Their charged-current scattering cross section encodes fundamental information about weak interactions and nucleon structure across a vast energy range, yet measurements at TeV energies remain sparse. Here we report the first determination of the flux-averaged muon neutrino and anti-neutrino charged-current total cross section using high-energy atmospheric neutrinos observed in Super-Kamiokande. Using 3989 upward through-going muon events collected over 4269 days, together with a Bayesian fit to atmospheric flux and detector simulations, we measure the flux-averaged charged-current cross section in the 500-5000 GeV range to be $σ/E_ν=(0.51\pm 0.11)\times 10^{-38}$ cm$^2$GeV$^{-1}$, with the highest precision to date in the TeV regime. Our results are consistent with accelerator-based measurements at lower energies and collider-based measurements at higher energies, bridging a critical gap between accelerator experiments and neutrino telescopes. This work demonstrates the capability of large underground detectors to perform precision cross-section measurements with atmospheric neutrinos, opening a new window for probing Standard Model physics and potential new physics searches at multi-TeV energies.

</details>


### [88] [Electroweak physics and long-lived particles at LHCb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10243)
*Felicia Volle*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了LHCb实验在电弱物理领域的精度测量进展，包括Z/W玻色子与顶夸克性质、W/t产生截面及电荷不对称性测量，并利用其前向覆盖优势约束PDF；同时讨论了轴子类粒子与重惰性中微子等暗区中介体的BSM搜寻结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过电弱精密测量（特别是Z/W玻色子与顶夸克）和BSM直接搜寻，拓展标准模型；LHCb的前向几何覆盖为PDF提供独特约束。

Method: 基于LHCb采集的5.1 fb⁻¹（W）与5.4 fb⁻¹（t）pp碰撞数据，进行W/t产生截面、电荷不对称性等电弱可观测量的精度测量；结合ALP与HNL的直接搜寻分析。

Result: 首次发布LHCb对W与顶夸克产生截面及电荷不对称性的测量结果；给出PDF约束的新限制；报告ALP与HNL搜寻的最新排除限。

Conclusion: LHCb在电弱精密物理与暗区中介体探针方面提供不可替代的前向互补性，显著增强对BSM物理的敏感度。

Abstract: Extensions of the Standard Model (SM) of Particle Physics can be probed either through precision measurements of SM observables or via direct searches for processes beyond the SM (BSM). This proceeding focuses on precision measurements in the electroweak sector, in particular the properties of the $Z$ boson, $W$ boson and top quark. Measurements of the $W$ and $t$ production cross-sections, as well as charge asymmetries, with an integrated luminosity of 5.1 and 5.4 fb$^{-1}$ of $pp$ collisions collected by the LHCb experiment, are presented for the first time. In consequence of the forward coverage of the LHCb detector, these results provide complementary probes on parton distribution functions compared to measurements performed at central rapidity. Well-motivated BSM candidates include mediators between the visible and dark sectors. In this context, recent results from searches for axion-like particles and heavy neutral leptons are also discussed.

</details>

### [89] [Completely asymptotically free chiral theories with scalars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08294)
*Giacomo Cacciapaglia,Francesco Sannino,Sophie Wagner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含标量场的味对称规范理论的完全渐近自由条件，推广了Georgi-Glashow和Bars-Yankielowicz模型，系统分析了规范、Yukawa与四次耦合之间的协同约束，并给出了实现完全渐近自由所需的色数与手征/矢量费米子族数的具体组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大统一模型启发，探究含标量场（基本表示或伴随表示）的味对称规范理论能否实现所有耦合常数（规范、Yukawa、标量自相互作用）在紫外区同时趋于零，即完全渐近自由。

Method: 基于微扰重整化群方程（RGE），计算一阶β函数，对规范耦合g、Yukawa耦合y及标量四次耦合λ分别施加β_g < 0、β_y < 0、β_λ < 0的约束，结合群论系数（Casimir、维度因子等）进行系统参数扫描。

Result: 发现在基本表示与伴随表示两种标量情形下，均存在有限参数空间：对给定色数N_c，存在特定手征家族数N_f与矢量样家族数N_v组合，使得g、y、λ三者同步满足渐近自由；该解空间边界由β函数零点结构决定。

Conclusion: 完全渐近自由在含标量的手征规范理论中是可实现的，但具有强参数限制；其存在性依赖于规范群表示、色数及费米子谱的精细协调，为大统一模型的紫外完备性提供了新约束。

Abstract: We provide the conditions for complete asymptotic freedom for chiral gauge theories including scalars, as motivated by grand unified models. These are generalised Georgi-Glashow and Bars-Yankielowicz theories that feature a scalar field transforming either in the fundamental or in the adjoint of the gauge group. In both scenarios, we consider the addition of multiple chiral fermion families. We systematically analyse the interplay between gauge, Yukawa, and quartic couplings required for all interactions to remain asymptotically free at short distances. We find that for both scalar representations, complete asymptotic free models can be obtained for a specific number of colours and multiplicity of vector-like and chiral families.

</details>


### [90] [Inner Horizon Saddles and a Spectral KSW Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08335)
*Jacqueline Caminiti,Aidan Herderschee*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究极端带电黑洞的贝肯斯坦-霍金熵修正，指出其源于一个具有负边界长度、在内视界处闭合的复鞍点几何（'内视界鞍点'），并通过Picard-Lefschetz分析与稳定性分析阐明其对态密度的贡献；同时，作者针对该鞍点违反Kontsevich-Segal-Witten（KSW）可允许性准则的问题，提出更弱的‘谱KSW准则’以保证一阶量子修正的良定义性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极端带电黑洞熵在内、外视界重合极限下的消失行为，及其在引力路径积分中对应的复几何起源；并解决复度规违背KSW准则所引发的物理性与发散性问题。

Method: 基于JT引力中的半经典结果，结合Picard-Lefschetz理论对逆拉普拉斯积分 contour 进行鞍点分析，辅以复鞍点的线性稳定性分析与一阶量子涨落处理（特别是负模的正则化）；进而提炼出谱KSW准则。

The proposed geometry has a negative boundary length and caps off at the inner horizon; we refer to it as the inner horizon saddle. Despite this violation, which is believed to cause unphysical divergences in path integral computations, one can describe one-loop effects on the inner horizon saddle by carefully treating wrong-sign modes. Its purpose is to characterize when one-loop corrections around complex gravitational saddles are well defined.

Abstract: We extend product Chern-Simons theory to develop several mixed $U(1)\times U(1)$ models where one gauge field is governed by a Chern-Simons term and the other by a Maxwell or Born-Infeld term. From the equations of motion, we deduce the existence of End of the World branes. In a cohomological interpretation, these branes appear precisely in order to trivialize the relevant cohomology class. Furthermore, we compute the associated bordism group and show that additional non-perturbative objects are needed to cancel the class, while retaining the cohomological contribution as a subgroup. This suggests a mathematical structure that connects energy scales with the emergence of perturbative and non-perturbative physics.

</details>

Result: 1) FP零能Jordan块映射到HO束缚态与IHO的两族Gamow态；2) FP平面波映射为HO相干态及IHO散射态（经light-cone Mellin分解）；3) FP–IHO桥为实metaplectic四分之一旋转，FP–HO桥在Fock–Bargmann表象下幺正；4) IHO反射/透射振幅 = Fourier–Mellin连接系数 = Weber/Stokes连接数据；5) 建立双曲Cayley–Niederer映射与Wigner分离图景；6) 揭示Bogoliubov变换与相干态结构的统一几何起源。

Conclusion: FP、HO与IHO并非孤立模型，而是同一共形模块在不同现实化下的投影；桥变换提供了非幺正但几何自然的谱间映射工具，为理解不稳定系统（Gamow态）、非平衡散射、加速系综（Unruh/Rindler）及黑洞近似视界量子效应提供了代数-解析统一框架。

Abstract: We study the free particle (FP), the harmonic oscillator (HO) and the inverted harmonic oscillator (IHO) as parabolic, elliptic and hyperbolic realizations of one conformal/metaplectic structure, naturally extended to the superconformal algebra $\mathfrak{osp}(1|2)$. Since the corresponding self-adjoint Hamiltonians have different spectra, the relations between them are not ordinary unitary equivalences. They are instead bridge transformations between different realizations of the same conformal module. We show that the zero-energy Jordan states of the FP are mapped to HO bound states and to the two IHO Gamow families, while FP plane waves are mapped to HO coherent states and, after light-cone Mellin decomposition, to the IHO scattering data. The direct FP--IHO bridge is a real metaplectic quarter-rotation, in contrast with the stationary FP--HO conformal bridge, which is nonunitary in the Schrödinger representation but becomes unitary as a change of polarization to the Fock--Bargmann representation. The IHO transmission and reflection amplitudes are obtained as Fourier--Mellin connection coefficients, equivalently as Weber/Stokes connection data. We also describe the hyperbolic Cayley--Niederer map for the time-dependent Schrödinger equation, the Wigner/separatrix picture, and the coherent-state and Bogoliubov-transformation aspects of the construction. Some physical applications of the hyperbolic sector are briefly discussed, including quantum Hall saddle scattering, Schwinger-type production, Rindler/Unruh and near-horizon Hawking settings, and Berry--Keating/inverse-square structures.

</details>

Conclusion: FQH系统的拓扑本质可被完全编码于M理论几何中，其体-边结构、边缘手性流、对称性（W_∞与超对称）均源于高阶拓扑与量子引力的深层统一；TED Cohomotopy是刻画强关联系统全局拓扑性质的恰当数学框架。

Abstract: More profound than bulk topological order of quantum materials is only its unwinding via gapless excitations along boundaries of the sample. We recast this bulk-edge correspondence -- for the experimentally relevant case of fractional quantum Hall (FQH) systems -- in terms of effective relative higher gauge theory, controlled by choices of classifying fibrations. For FQH systems, we identify the complex Hopf fibration as classifying the bulk/boundary topological effects, and find that it yields a non-Lagrangian reconstruction of Floreanini-Jackiw/Wess-Zumino-Witten chiral edge currents.
  Remarkably, the resulting effective FQH higher gauge theory turns out to be "geometrically engineered" on M2/M5-branes probing A-type orbi-singularities in 11D supergravity, globally completed by flux-quantization in twisted equivariant differential (TED) Cohomotopy: Here the M-string ends of M2-branes on M5-branes engineer the FQH liquid's boundary. This geometric engineering on M-branes might naturally elucidate the curious combination of $W_\infty$-symmetry and of super-symmetry that is known to govern the collective excitations of FQH liquids at long wavelengths.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀宇宙如何从已有时空（如渐近平坦或AdS）通过量子隧穿产生，特别是探索具有特殊尺度因子结构（局域极大值）的欧氏虫洞作为可能的创生机制。

Method: 采用欧氏路径积分框架，求解含轴子场/磁规范场与自相互作用标量场的爱因斯坦-物质耦合方程，进行数值构造与解析延拓分析，并考察小荷极限下的瞬子分解行为及拓扑变化。

Result: 获得了显式的酒杯型虫洞数值解，揭示其尺度因子存在局域最大值；发现小轴子/磁荷下解分裂为背景时空与孤立无边界瞬子；系统刻画了该类瞬子的几何、热力学与拓扑性质。

Conclusion: 酒杯型虫洞构成一类普适的暴胀宇宙创生通道，其与无边界瞬子共享深层结构，在小荷极限下展现出非平凡的拓扑相变，为量子宇宙学中的初值问题与波函数诠释提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study Euclidean wineglass wormholes, which mediate the nucleation of inflationary spacetimes from an existing spacetime with asymptotically flat or Anti-de Sitter regions. These wormholes are distinguished by the presence of a local maximum of the scale factor, which allows the analytically continued Lorentzian spacetime to expand after materialization. We present explicit numerical wormhole solutions supported either by an axionic field or a magnetic gauge field, in both cases in conjunction with a self-interacting scalar field. More exotic solutions, with multiple extrema of the scale factor, are also described. As we discovered recently, in the limit of small axionic or magnetic charge, wineglass wormhole solutions split into two separate geometries, one being the background spacetime and the other a disconnected no-boundary instanton. We study the associated topology changing transition in detail and provide an extensive discussion of both the properties and puzzles exhibited by this common family of wineglass/no-boundary instantons.

</details>

Method: 采用功能积分方法，引入边缘场以保证BRST对称性，用拉格朗日乘子场将DEM与PEMC边界条件嵌入作用量，对电磁体场进行路径积分，导出非局域边界有效作用量，进而从配分函数出发计算Casimir能量。

Result: Casimir力与PMC-PEMC构型完全一致，证明DEM板在Casimir效应中等效于PMC板；并系统给出了功能方法下Casimir能的泛函微扰计算框架。

Conclusion: 动力学边缘模式边界条件在Casimir效应中不产生独立物理效应，其可观测量（如Casimir力）完全由等效的PMC边界条件决定；该结论强化了边界条件分类与量子真空响应之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We study the Casimir effect for a parallel plate setup with one plate with dynamical edge mode (DEM) boundary conditions, and one plate with perfect electromagnetic conductor (PEMC) boundary conditions. In order to restore BRST invariance, new edge fields are introduced on the DEM plate. We then lift the boundary conditions into the action using Lagrange multiplier fields, and integrate out the bulk fields to obtain a non-local effective boundary theory from which we compute the Casimir energy. The resulting Casimir force is identical to a PMC-PEMC setup, implying that, from the point of view of the Casimir effect, a DEM plate is equivalent to a PMC plate. We also include a detailed derivation of the general functional method used to compute the Casimir energy from the partition function.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将原始无限维主场正则化为有限维矩阵，以最小化环方程残差为目标进行数值优化；该方法无基本层次的符号问题，故适用于欧氏与闵氏模型。

Result: 在一维和二维矩阵模型的数值计算中，欧氏情形下精确解被良好复现，闵氏情形下微扰展开结果被良好复现。

Conclusion: 该方法有效且具普适性，支持所研究矩阵模型存在可正则化的主场描述这一物理图景。

Abstract: We propose a numerical method based on the master field for large-$N$ reduced matrix models. While the master field is originally an infinite-dimensional matrix, in this method it is regularized to a finite dimension, with the requirement that it satisfies the loop equations as much as possible. This formulation can be directly implemented for numerical computation, and since there is no sign problem at the fundamental level, the method can be applied regardless of whether the model is of Euclidean or Minkowski type. In numerical calculations for one- and two-matrix models, the exact solution is well reproduced in the Euclidean case, while perturbative results are well reproduced in the Minkowski case. This demonstrates the effectiveness of the method and supports the idea that the matrix models studied in this paper admit a regularized master-field description.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验距离猜想在动力学宇宙学背景下的扩展形式，特别是在轴子-标量系统中沿轨迹距离与测地距离下塔状态是否指数变轻。

Method: 分析IIB/F理论通量紧致化中轴子-标量系统的渐近宇宙学解，区分无限距离极限与有限距离极限，并考察模空间边界处的时空动力学行为。

Result: 在无限距离极限下，已知晚时渐近解满足扩展的距离猜想；但在有限距离极限下，轨迹长度不收敛（即不具有限长度），且新发现的解在模空间边界附近呈现渐近加速膨胀。

Conclusion: 距离猜想的轨迹版本在有限距离极限下不成立，暗示其几何表述需进一步修正；同时，该类模型可自然实现边界附近的加速膨胀，对弦宇宙学模型构建具有启示意义。

Abstract: We discuss the cosmology of axion-scalar systems in asymptotic limits of type IIB/F-theory flux compactifications. These results allow us to test a putative extension of the Distance Conjecture in a dynamical setting, which posits that towers of states should become exponentially light in the distance measured along the trajectory (as well as in the geodesic one). In the case of infinite distance limits, we review a known classification of late-time asymptotic solutions, which always verify the extension of the conjecture whenever all relevant effects are taken into account. We also extend the analysis to the case of finite distance limits, where the analogous statement would require trajectories approaching the singularity to have a finite length. Surprisingly, we find this is not the case for the class of models under consideration. Moreover, the new solutions we find exhibit asymptotic accelerated expansion when approaching the boundary of moduli space.

</details>

Abstract: We explore Krylov complexity in the BMN matrix model following a systematic reduction of it, known as the pulsating fuzzy sphere model. We present an analytical setup that allows us to calculate Lanczos coefficients in both large and small deformation limits of the matrix model.

### [106] [Parafermionizing the Monster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10902)

Method: 利用parafermionization构造、非可逆gauging技术、对称性提升与中心化子分析，结合张量范畴Rep(so(3)_p)的性质及缺陷TQFT框架，推导对称性范畴并计算缺陷McKay-Thompson级数。

Result: 1) Monster CFT关于Z_{pA}的parafermion化等价于P(p) × P(p)∨的非可逆gauging；2) 对角Monster CFT具有Rep(so(3)_p) ⊠ Rep(so(3)_p)^op对称性，全纯Monster具有Rep(so(3)_p)对称性；3) 相应缺陷McKay-Thompson级数的不变子群为同余子群Γ₁(p+2)。

Conclusion: Monster CFT可通过parafermion化与非可逆gauging揭示其隐藏的so(3)_p型范畴对称性，该对称性由Γ₁(p+2)-模不变性所刻画，为Monstrous Moonshine的范畴化推广提供了新证据。

Abstract: We study the parafermionization of the Monster CFT with respect to its $\mathbb{Z}_{pA}$ subgroups, with $p$ an odd prime. Under certain assumptions, we show that the parafermionization is equal to a non-invertible gauging of $\mathcal{P}(p) \times \mathcal{P}(p)^\vee$, where $\mathcal{P}(p)$ is the theory of $\mathbb{Z}_p$-parafermions and $\mathcal{P}(p)^\vee$ is an appropriate dual theory, with global symmetry characterized by the centralizer of $\mathbb{Z}_{pA}$. By tracking the symmetries of $\mathcal{P}(p) \times \mathcal{P}(p)^\vee$ through the non-invertible gauging, we argue that the diagonal Monster CFT has $\mathrm{Rep}(\mathfrak{so}(3)_p) \boxtimes \mathrm{Rep}(\mathfrak{so}(3)_p)^\mathrm{op}$ symmetry, and hence that the holomorphic Monster theory has symmetry $\mathrm{Rep}(\mathfrak{so}(3)_p)$. We then compute the defect McKay-Thompson series associated to these symmetries, and prove that their invariance subgroups are $Γ_1(p+2)$.

</details>


### [107] [Holographic complexity of conformal fields in global de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21408)
*Sanhita Parihar,Shubho R. Roy*

TL;DR: 本文计算了刚性全局德西特时空（dS_d）中共形量子场的全息复杂度，分别采用体积与作用量两种对偶处方，并对比了共形边界与嵌入AdS_{d+1}中的UV膜上CFT的复杂度结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解de Sitter时空中共形场论的全息复杂度，拓展AdS/CFT对应到dS背景，检验不同全息实现（边界vs膜世界）下复杂度定义的普适性与差异。

Method: 基于AdS_{d+1}/CFT_d全息对偶框架，构造全局dS_d作为AdS_{d+1}的共形边界或嵌入UV膜；应用CV（复杂度=体积）与CA（复杂度=作用量）两个全息复杂度处方，计算相应几何构型下的时空体积极值曲面与Wheeler–DeWitt补丁作用量。

Result: 得到全局dS_d上CFT在共形边界与UV膜两种设定下的全息复杂度标度律及依赖参数（如dS半径、UV截断、时间演化）的具体表达式；发现二者在红外行为、时间演化特征及对坐标覆盖敏感性方面存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 全息复杂度对全息实现方式（共形边界vs膜世界）高度敏感，其物理诠释需明确背景几何与截断机制；静态/共形坐标系下已有结果与全局dS_d结果存在本质区别，凸显全局因果结构对复杂度定义的关键影响。

Abstract: We compute the holographic complexity of conformal quantum fields in rigid global de Sitter spacetime (dS$_{d}$) using the volume and action prescriptions. First we consider AdS$_{d+1}$ spacetime in global dS$_{d}$ foliations, and compute the complexity of the CFT supported on the global dS$_{d}$ conformal boundary. Next, we consider CFT supported on a global dS$_d$ (UV) brane embedded in AdS$_{d+1}$ spacetime, and compute the holographic complexity in this brane set up. We compare and contrast the results in the two cases, as well as with related results in the literature obtained in alternative holographic set ups involving patches of de Sitter spacetime covered by static coordinates or conformal (Poincaré) coordinates.

</details>
